### [1] [Hunyuan3D-Buffalo 1.0: A Unified Multimodal Model for Scalable 3D Generation, Understanding, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02711)
*Junliang Ye,Kenkun Liu,Guocun Wang,Yang Li,Yansong Qu,Chunshi Wang,Jingwei Xu,Yunhan Yang,Zibo Zhao,Jiachen Xu,Jiaao Yu,Lifu Wang,Zhihao Liang,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hunyuan3D-Buffalo 1.0 是一个统一的3D多模态框架，将3D理解、文生3D、指令引导的3D编辑和文本驱动的部件生成整合在单一架构中，并通过构建8700万规模的3D多模态语料库（含2500万理解样本、5000万文生3D对和1200万编辑对）实现可扩展训练，在文生3D和3D编辑基准上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一多模态模型在图像领域已取得显著进展，但统一的3D建模因缺乏大规模且几何一致的编辑数据而受限。为了解决3D多模态数据稀缺的问题，作者提出构建大规模3D多模态语料库并设计统一架构来同时支持3D理解、生成与编辑。

Method: 该框架由三个关键组件构成：(1) 构建8700万规模的3D多模态语料库，包含2500万理解样本、5000万文生3D对和1200万编辑对，其中编辑对通过Nano3D-v2生成；(2) 架构上结合Hunyuan3D-VLM（提供语义、结构和空间理解）与Hunyuan3D DiT（进行高保真3D合成），VLM为生成提供多模态语义条件；(3) 编辑和部件生成任务额外以源对象表示作为扩散过程的条件，以保持整体结构和未编辑区域的一致性。

Result: 实验表明Hunyuan3D-Buffalo 1.0在文生3D和3D编辑基准上达到最优或领先性能，同时展现出强大的3D理解和部件生成能力。分析与消融进一步显示生成和理解能力共同提升了编辑效果，验证了统一3D多模态训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一3D多模态训练是可行的且有效的：通过在单一架构中整合理解、生成和编辑，并借助大规模数据增强，各任务之间能够实现相互促进，从而突破3D编辑数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have demonstrated the potential of unified multimodal models that integrate understanding, generation, and editing. However, unified 3D modeling remains constrained by scarce multimodal data, particularly the lack of large-scale and geometrically consistent editing data. To address this limitation, we propose Hunyuan3D-Buffalo 1.0, a unified framework supporting 3D understanding, text-to-3D generation, instruction-guided 3D editing, and text-grounded part generation within a single architecture. To enable scalable training, we construct an 87M-scale 3D multimodal corpus, comprising 25M understanding samples, 50M text-to-3D pairs, and 12M editing pairs generated using Nano3D-v2. Architecturally, the framework combines Hunyuan3D-VLM for semantic, structural, and spatial understanding with Hunyuan3D DiT for high-fidelity 3D synthesis. The VLM provides multimodal semantic conditions for generation, while editing and part generation additionally condition the diffusion process on the source object representation to preserve its overall structure and unedited regions. Extensive experiments show that Hunyuan3D-Buffalo 1.0 achieves state-of-the-art or leading performance on text-to-3D generation and 3D editing benchmarks, while exhibiting strong understanding and part-generation capabilities. Our analysis further shows that both generation and understanding improve editing, demonstrating the effectiveness of unified 3D multimodal training. Project Page: https://tencent-hunyuan.github.io/Hunyuan3D-Buffalo1.0/

</details>


### [2] [Quo Vadis, World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02713)
*Yu Yang,Xuemeng Yang,Licheng Wen,Lingdong Kong,Xiaobin Hu,Dongyue Lu,Wei Chow,Xiyan Huang,Yuxiang Feng,Yue Liao,Jianbiao Mei,Daocheng Fu,Rong Wu,Pinlong Cai,Ran Yi,Ying Tai,Jiangning Zhang,Botian Shi,Yong Liu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-Centric Interactive World Proxies（代理中心交互式世界代理）这一新范式，将世界建模从传统的物理状态转移预测转向代理可用信息转移（如执行结果、检索经验、技能、验证信号），为持续改进的代理提供多种反馈形式。文章将世界代理分为六种功能形态（动力学、空间、执行、记忆/经验、技能、奖励/验证），并分析了它们在三个渐进层级（推理时引导、训练时优化、代理-代理共同进化）中赋能代理的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续改进的代理需要动态交互反馈而非静态监督，但直接真实环境交互成本高、速度慢、不安全、难以并行化。经典世界模型仅通过未来物理状态预测来实例化代理交互的代理，这种表述对需要超越原始状态转换的可操作反馈的代理而言过于狭窄。因此需要扩展世界建模以提供更通用的反馈。

Method: 提出了Agent-Centric Interactive World Proxies的概念，将世界建模范式从物理状态转移转变为代理可用信息转移。通过系统化映射设计空间，将世界代理按反馈模态组织为六种功能形式：动力学、空间、执行、记忆/经验、技能和奖励/验证代理。并分析这些代理在三个渐进层面赋能代理：L.1推理时引导、L.2训练时优化、L.3代理-代理共同进化。

Result: 建立了一个系统化的世界代理设计空间分类框架（6种功能形式×3个赋能层级），将世界建模重新定位为代理中心范式，提出了从基于状态转换到基于代理可用信息转换的范式转移路线图。

Conclusion: 该工作重新定义了世界建模为代理中心的范式，建立了构建世界代理的路线图，使代理能够更好地规划、更快地学习、持续进化。这是对未来世界模型研究方向的范式性贡献，拓展了世界建模的能力边界，使其超越了传统的物理状态预测范畴。

Abstract: Continually improving agents require dynamic interaction feedback beyond static supervision, yet direct real-environment interaction is costly, slow, unsafe, and hard to parallelize. World modeling offers a natural intermediate proxy that allows agents to query lower-cost, more controllable feedback before committing to real actions. Classical world models instantiate this proxy primarily through future physical-state prediction, a formulation useful yet narrow for agents that require actionable feedback beyond raw state transitions. In this work, we conceptualize Agent-Centric Interactive World Proxies, shifting the fundamental paradigm from physical state transitions to agent-usable information transitions, such as execution outcomes, retrieved experiences or skills, and verification signals, broadening the scope of world modeling to provide versatile feedback for continually improving agents. To systematically map this design space, we organize world proxies into six functional forms based on their feedback modalities: dynamics, spatial, execution, memory/experience, skill, and reward/verification proxies, which together characterize the primary ways world modeling serves agent improvement. We further analyze how these proxies empower agents across three progressive levels: L.1 Inference-Time Guidance, where proxy outputs enrich in-context information for superior decisions; L.2 Training-Time Optimization, where proxy outputs yield rewards, critiques, or synthetic rollouts for policy learning; and L.3 Agent-Proxy Co-Evolution, where real-environment evidence continuously updates both the proxy and the agent for co-evolution. Ultimately, this work recasts world modeling into an agent-centric paradigm, establishing a roadmap for building world proxies that empower agents to plan better, learn faster, and evolve continually.

</details>


### [3] [Oh Deer, How Should I Handle This? Seasonal Priors for Selective Wildlife Annotation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02762)
*Hugo Markoff,Christoph Praschl,Anton Hjalte Jørgensen,Christian Emil Mogensen,Mathias Bech Skadhauge,Sara Beery,Michael Ørsted,David C. Schedl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究航拍图像中野生动物细粒度分类的标签不可靠问题，以红鹿成年雄性识别为例，利用鹿角周期这一生物学季节性结构，将标注质量、下游分类和选择性预测三者联系起来。结合RGB+热成像双模态审核可恢复单一模态遗漏的多数雄性标签，不确定性区间的弃权策略将覆盖精度提升至98.9%，最终证明生物学季历可预测标注与预测的不可靠区域，并指导标注协议设计与模态加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像中的细粒度野生动物分类受限于模型性能之外，标签本身也不可靠——动物仅占据少量像素、关键视觉线索随季节变化、单模态证据存在歧义。作者旨在利用生物学先验（红鹿的鹿角周期）刻画可靠的证据窗口，以同时改善标注质量和预测可靠性。

Method: 使用7,295个RGB-only、thermal-only以及匹配的RGB+thermal裁剪样本（由三名标注者标注），研究季节性结构对标注质量、下游分类和选择性预测三者的影响。对比单模态与双模态标注/分类表现，引入软季节性先验，并采用基于不确定性区间的弃权（abstention）策略提升覆盖精度。

Result: 匹配的RGB+thermal双模态审核比任一单一模态解决更多样本，可恢复RGB或热成像单独无法识别的多数雄性标签（人类与模型分类均验证）；高标注弃权月份对应低分类器置信度；软季节性先验主要惠及受季节性限制的热成像视图；不确定性弃权将覆盖精度提升至98.9%，但牺牲覆盖度且弃权不成比例地落在雄性样本上。

Conclusion: 生物学驱动的季节性日历能够预测标注与预测的不可靠性时段，可用以指导标注协议设计（如双模态审核时机）和模态权重分配（如季节先验），为野生动物视觉识别中的标签可靠性问题提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained wildlife classification in aerial imagery is limited not only by model performance, but also by unreliable labels: animals occupy few pixels, key visual cues vary seasonally, and modality-specific evidence can be ambiguous. We study adult-male identification in red deer, where the antler cycle defines predictable windows of reliable evidence for both annotation and prediction. Using 7,295 RGB-only, thermal-only, and matched RGB+thermal crop sets labeled by three annotators, we show that seasonal structure links (I) annotation quality, (II) downstream classification, and (III) selective prediction. Matched RGB+thermal review resolves more samples than either single modality, recovering majority-male labels otherwise missed by RGB or thermal alone, in human based as well as model based classification. Months with high annotator abstention also show lower classifier confidence, and soft seasonal priors mainly benefit the season-limited thermal view. Uncertainty-band abstention further improves covered accuracy up to 98.9%, though at reduced coverage and with deferral that falls disproportionately on males. Overall, a biologically grounded seasonal calendar predicts where annotation and prediction are unreliable, and can guide both annotation protocol design and modality weighting.

</details>


### [4] [Confident but Unreliable: A Behavioral Safety Audit of Vision-Language Models on Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02790)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Mohammadsajad Abavisani,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以脑部MRI为受控测试平台，对六个指令微调视觉语言模型（VLM）进行行为审计，发现这些模型在回答覆盖率高、准确率看似可接受的情况下，其口头表述的置信度校准极差（ECE高达0.27-0.40），平均错误置信度达0.82-0.97，33-46%的回答是高置信度错误。研究建议医学影像VLM评估应同时报告置信度可靠性、自信错误、幻觉和弃答率，而不只是准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像VLM研究通常将正确性与模型表达的置信度混为一谈或仅关注准确率，缺乏对模型"自我认知"的独立评估。这种疏忽可能导致模型表面"胜任"而实际缺乏可靠的自知之明，在高风险的医学决策场景中尤为危险。

Method: 使用4,102张图像（4,032张来自250名受试者的轴向/冠状/矢状MRI切片及70张非脑/噪声对照），标签来自公共元数据和已发布的专家分割掩码而非人工新标注。对六个指令微调VLM（五个通用+一个医学专用）进行自动评分的"行为审计"，评测多维能力：答案覆盖率、口头置信度校准（ECE）、错误置信度、高置信度错误比例、幻觉和弃答行为，并对比基础模型与医学专用模型系列。

Result: 所有模型的回答覆盖率接近完整，但置信度校准普遍较差：ECE从0.27到0.40，错误答案的平均置信度从0.82到0.97，33-46%的回答为高置信度错误。最准确的模型在错误上也最自信；医学适配（医学专用模型）改善了肿瘤存在检测但未改善置信度可靠性。开放问答诊断显示幻觉和弃答与多项选择的准确率独立变化。

Conclusion: 医学图像VLM评估不能仅看准确率，必须同时报告口头置信度可靠性、自信错误率、幻觉率和弃答率。现有VLM（包括医学专用模型）在自我认知方面存在系统性缺陷，需要建立更全面的评估标准以保障高风险的医学应用安全。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), including medical specialists, are increasingly proposed for medical imaging, yet their stated confidence is rarely evaluated separately from correctness. We use brain MRI as a controlled, high-stakes testbed for a broader failure mode in frontier multimodal systems: models can appear competent while lacking reliable self-knowledge. We present an automatically graded behavioral audit and pilot study of six instruction-tuned VLMs (five general-purpose and one medical specialist) on 4,102 images (4,032 axial/coronal/sagittal MRI slices from 250 subjects plus 70 non-brain/noise controls), with labels derived from public metadata and released expert segmentation masks rather than new human annotation.
  Across models, answer coverage is near-complete, but verbalized-confidence calibration is poor: ECE ranges from 0.27 to 0.40, mean confidence on incorrect answers ranges from 0.82 to 0.97, and 33-46% of answered items are high-confidence errors. The most accurate model is also the most confident on its errors, while a base/specialist family contrast suggests that medical adaptation improves tumor-presence detection without improving confidence reliability. Open-ended diagnostics further show that hallucination and abstention vary separately from multiple-choice accuracy. These findings argue that medical-image VLM evaluation should report verbalized-confidence reliability, confident error, hallucination, and abstention alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [Better, Stronger, Faster, and Broader: Structured All-Mask Prediction for MLLM-Based Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02791)
*Jiazhen Liu,Mingkuan Feng,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出All-Mask Prediction框架以解决多模态大模型（MLLM）分割中的"分割三难困境"（高性能分割、保持对话能力、快速推理）。其二元实例STAMP通过触发词和混合注意力一次性完成二分类掩码预测；进一步提出结构化版本STAMPlus，通过目标列表和共享多类掩码空间实现单次非自回归的多目标预测，在多种分割任务上达到SOTA性能，同时保留多模态对话能力并将12类别推理延迟从13.50s降至5.16s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM分割方法面临三难困境：嵌入预测方法通过像素级目标可能破坏语言建模能力，而逐词生成方法对密集掩码推理效率低下。需要一个能够同时兼顾分割性能、对话能力和推理效率的统一框架。

Method: 提出All-Mask Prediction框架，将自回归对话与非自回归掩码预测解耦。STAMP（二元实例）通过发射词汇表内<SEG>触发词，融合图像对齐的掩码token与对应patch特征，使用混合注意力一次性将所有token分类为前景或背景。STAMPlus（结构化版本）生成带显式ID和可选边界框的目标列表，将ID绑定到共享多类掩码空间，在单次非自回归前向传播中联合预测所有目标，并通过高分辨率掩码token缩放保留细微空间证据以支持小目标分割。

Result: STAMPlus在指代分割、推理分割、开放词汇语义分割、实例感知分割和遥感小目标分割等场景均达到SOTA性能，保留通用多模态指令跟随能力，并将12类别推理延迟从重复STAMP推理的13.50s降至5.16s。进一步分析表明准确的目标线索有助于分割，学到的空间定位有助于"再看一眼"推理。

Conclusion: STAMPlus通过结构化All-Mask Prediction成功在生产、超出单目标预测，解决了MLLM分割的三难困境，在保持对话能力和高效推理的同时实现了领先的分割性能。

Abstract: MLLM-based segmentation faces a core segmentation trilemma: high segmentation performance, preserved dialogue ability, and fast inference. Embedding-prediction methods may disrupt language modeling through pixel-level objectives, whereas next-token generation is inefficient for dense masks. We propose All-Mask Prediction, decoupling autoregressive dialogue from non-autoregressive mask prediction. Its binary instantiation, STAMP (Simultaneous Textual All-Mask Prediction), emits an in-vocabulary <SEG> trigger, fuses image-aligned mask tokens with corresponding patch features, and uses hybrid attention to classify all tokens as foreground or background in one pass. It thereby combines strong referring and reasoning segmentation with preserved multimodal ability and efficient inference. However, binary masks cannot retain multiple semantic or instance identities without repeated target-specific predictions. We therefore propose Structured All-Mask Prediction and develop STAMPlus. It generates a target list with explicit IDs and optional boxes, binds these IDs to a shared multi-class mask space, and jointly predicts all targets in one non-autoregressive pass. A single unified checkpoint retains STAMP's referring and reasoning capabilities while extending to open-vocabulary semantic, instance-aware, and remote-sensing small-target segmentation, where high-resolution mask-token scaling preserves finer spatial evidence. Across these settings, STAMPlus achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance, preserves general multimodal instruction following, and reduces 12-category latency from 13.50s for repeated STAMP inference to 5.16s. Further analyses show that accurate target cues improve segmentation and learned spatial grounding benefits look-twice reasoning. Overall, STAMPlus resolves the trilemma beyond single-target prediction.

</details>


### [6] [PixelUp: Zero-Shot Semantic Feature Upsampling for Fine-Grained Vision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02792)
*Deepank Singh,Anurag Nihal,Vedhus Hoskere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelUp是一种零样本、与视觉基础模型（VFM）无关的特征上采样器，通过粗到细的窗口交叉注意力链结合多尺度语义特征引导，解决VFM patch-token特征在密集预测任务中粒度不足的问题，在语义分割和深度估计等任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视觉基础模型（VFM）的patch-token级特征过于粗糙，难以满足语义分割、深度估计等密集预测任务对像素级细节的需求。现有特征上采样方法存在两大局限：可学习上采样器通常为特定编码器定制，需针对不同编码器重新训练；图像引导方法依赖浅层像素编码器，容易引入纹理伪影且缺乏语义引导，导致下游预测不准确。

Method: PixelUp采用粗到细的窗口交叉注意力链架构，利用多尺度语义特征进行引导，实现语义感知的上采样。该方法为零样本且VFM无关，即无需针对特定编码器重新训练即可直接应用于不同的VFM。

Result: PixelUp在密集预测任务上超越了VFM专用和VFM无关的上采样器并取得SOTA性能：语义分割平均提升+1.2 mIoU，NYUv2深度估计平均提升+0.25 δ1（跨多个VFM）。此外，在无需训练的开放词汇语义分割中平均提升+1.3 mIoU，在无监督语义分割中平均提升+0.5 mIoU。

Conclusion: 实验证明PixelUp作为零样本、VFM无关的语义感知上采样器，能有效克服现有方法的局限性，在多种密集预测任务和不同VFM上均取得一致的性能提升，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have become essential backbones for downstream tasks due to their strong and transferable visual representations. However, their patch-token-level features are often too coarse for dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation and depth estimation when accurate fine-grained predictions are required. Feature upsampling methods have been developed to recover pixel-level detail but still face limitations. Learnable upsamplers are often designed for a specific encoders and must be retrained for different encoders. Image-guided methods that use shallow pixel encoders often introduce textural artifacts and lack the semantic guidance needed for accurate downstream predictions. We introduce PixelUp, a zero-shot VFM-agnostic upsampler achieving semantic awareness through a coarse-to-fine chain of windowed cross-attention architecture guided by multi-scale semantic features. We demonstrate that PixelUp outperforms both VFM-specific and VFM-agnostic upsamplers, achieving state-of-the-art performance on dense prediction tasks with an average improvement of +1.2 mIoU on semantic segmentation and +0.25 $δ_1$, on NYUv2 depth estimation across VFMs. PixelUp further improves training-free open-vocabulary and unsupervised semantic segmentation by an average of +1.3 mIoU and +0.5 mIoU, respectively. Code available at https://pixelup-project.vercel.app/

</details>


### [7] [SAGE: Semantic Explainability of Attention-Based Survival Models in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02803)
*Abdallah Lamane,Abdul Rahman Diab,Ren-Chin Wu,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE是一个事后解释框架，从冻结的ABMIL模型中提取全局、语言化的语义解释。它利用病理学视觉-语言模型，用25个组织学概念对图像块打分，并按模型学到的注意力聚合这些分数，量化各概念与队列中预测风险的关系。在7个TCGA癌症队列和3个基础模型上的生存预测实验中，SAGE成功恢复了已知预后特征并揭示了癌症特异性生物学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABMIL是计算病理学中切片级预测的主流方法，但其注意力图仅提供局部解释：只能指出模型关注的位置，无法说明哪些组织学特征驱动预测，也无法展示模型在整个患者队列上的行为。缺乏全局的、语义层面的可解释性工具。

Method: SAGE框架包含三步：1) 使用病理视觉-语言模型将图像块映射到25个组织学概念的字典上进行打分；2) 根据模型学到的注意力权重聚合这些概念分数；3) 量化每个概念与队列层面预测风险之间的关联。该方法为事后、模型无关的框架。

Result: 在7个TCGA癌症队列和3种基础模型上，SAGE恢复了已确立的预后特征（如坏死与不良预后的关联），同时发现了癌症特异性生物学（如肾细胞癌中与已知分子亚型一致的良好血管生成特征）。消融实验表明这些关联依赖模型学到的注意力而非概念的普遍出现频率，且概念字典捕获了基础模型特征中编码的大部分预后信息。

Conclusion: SAGE通过语义化的全局解释，为理解ABMIL生存模型的学习内容提供了一个可扩展、模型无关的框架，使病理学家能够从队列层面解读模型行为，并具备生物标志物发现的潜力。

Abstract: Attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) is the predominant approach for slide-level prediction in computational pathology, yet its attention maps provide only local explanations: they indicate where a model focuses but not which histological features drive its predictions or how the model behaves across a patient cohort. We present Semantic Attention Global Explanations (SAGE), a post-hoc framework that extracts global, language-grounded explanations from a frozen ABMIL model. Using a pathology vision-language model, SAGE scores image patches against a dictionary of 25 histological concepts, aggregates these scores according to the model's learned attention, and quantifies how each concept relates to prediction risk across a cohort. Applied to survival prediction using seven TCGA cancer cohorts and three foundation models, SAGE recovered established prognostic features, such as the adverse association of necrosis, while revealing cancer-specific biology, including a favorable angiogenic signature in renal cell carcinoma consistent with known molecular subtypes. Ablation studies demonstrated that these associations depend on the model's learned attention rather than concept prevalence alone, and that the concept dictionary captures much of the prognostic information encoded by the foundation model features. Through semantically-grounded explanations, SAGE provides a scalable, model-agnostic framework for understanding what ABMIL survival models learn, enabling pathologists to interpret model behavior at the cohort level and offering the potential for biomarker identification.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 开发了一个统一的2D病灶分析框架，集成了基于LLM的推理、病灶边界框检测、分割以及影像报告生成四个模块。使用原始DeepLesion数据集，并整合空间与解剖学上下文到短文报告生成流程中。

Conclusion: 该框架在原始DeepLesion数据集上有效解决了病灶分割这一难题，显著优于nnUNet基线，同时实现了多任务病灶分析能力，并已开源发布。

Abstract: In previous work, we integrated large language models (LLMs) into the lesion segmentation model based on the ULS23 DeepLesion dataset, using short-form findings from the reports. In this study, we developed a unified 2D lesion analysis framework that integrates LLM-based reasoning, lesion bounding box detection, segmentation, and radiology report generation from the original DeepLesion dataset. In the testing phase, we achieved relatively high lesion bounding box detection accuracy with mAP50 of 70.1%, mAP50-95 of 46.4%; Lesion segmentation performance with a Dice score of 62.6%; short report generation accuracy with BLEU_1 score of 64.3%, BLEU_4 score of 49.6%, METEOR of 34.7%, and ROUGE_L of 60.1%. In this work, we address the challenging issue of segmentation in the original DeepLesion dataset and achieve a 28.5% Dice score improvement over the nnUNet lesion segmentation model. We also integrated spatial and anatomical context into the DeepLesion short report generation. We released the implementation, dataset, and models on Github. https://github.com/ruida/2D_DeepLesion_Foundation

</details>


### [9] [In-Context Collapse in Vision-Language Models and How to Mitigate it?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02830)
*Mohammad Rostami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在多样本上下文学习（ICL）中的一个反直觉现象——"上下文坍缩"（in-context collapse）：随着演示样本增加，部分VLM的准确率不升反降，甚至低于随机水平。通过参数匹配的消融与拯救实验，作者将坍缩定位到视觉-语言整合通路（connector及早期/中层），并提出CircA（一次性集成疫苗），在单一合成任务上训练即可将坍缩抗性迁移到未见任务族。关键发现是：擅长上下文整合的层并非擅长权重更新的层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设多样本ICL会随着演示数量增加而持续提升VLM性能，但作者观察到部分模型出现不升反降的异常现象。这一反直觉行为挑战了领域共识，且其根源不明。理解并修复这一"上下文坍缩"对于VLM的可扩展部署和可靠性至关重要。

Method: (1) 在多个VLM（0.5B-11B开源模型及Claude Sonnet 4.5）上进行系统性多样本ICL实验，覆盖合成分类、自然图像分类和VQA基准。(2) 区分"累积演示鲁棒性"和"上下文学习新规则"两种能力，识别出三种可复现的行为机制。(3) 通过参数匹配的消融-拯救实验进行因果定位，比较在connector/早期中层vs晚期readout上加adapter的效果。(4) 提出CircA：在单一合成任务上一次训练"集成疫苗"，迁移坍缩抗性到未知任务族。

Result: (1) 确认了上下文坍缩现象在多个VLM中呈梯度式（graded）出现，部分模型性能断崖式下降甚至低于随机。(2) 因果定位表明坍缩本质是视觉-语言整合路径的失败：在connector及早期/中层加adapter可将16-shot remap准确率从0.39提升至0.91，而同容量的late readout adapter无效。(3) CircA疫苗在CIFAR/Fashion上实现从随机水平到0.71/0.60的迁移抗性。(4) 晚期readout层在权重更新场景下表现更优且遗忘更少，说明上下文整合与权重巩固需要不同的层定位。

Conclusion: 多样本ICL中的上下文坍缩是一个可诊断、可修复的视觉-语言接口整合失败，而非模型容量或过拟合问题。通过轻量级、可迁移的干预（CircA），可以在未知任务族上恢复坍缩抗性。该工作为VLM的ICL可靠性设计提供了新方向，并揭示了上下文内整合与权重更新两种学习机制在层定位上的分离。

Abstract: Many-shot in-context learning (ICL) lets vision-language models (VLMs) adapt from image--label demonstrations without weight updates, and is widely assumed to improve as more demonstrations are supplied. We show the opposite: as demonstrations accumulate, a subset of VLMs undergo an \emph{in-context collapse}, a sharp, sometimes catastrophic accuracy drop spanning synthetic classification, natural-image classification, and VQA benchmarks, in some models falling below chance while outputs remain well-formed. Across an open VLM panel ($0.5$B--$11$B) and a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.5), the collapse is graded. Two capabilities turn out to be dissociable: robustness to accumulating demonstrations and the ability to learn a novel rule in context, their combinations yield three reproducible regimes. A parameter-matched lesion-and-rescue causally localizes the collapse to the vision-language integration pathway: an adapter on the connector and early/mid layers restores genuine learning (remap accuracy $0.39!\rightarrow!0.91$ at 16 shots), while an equal-capacity adapter on the late readout does not. We propose \textsc{CircA}, whose core is a one-time integration vaccine: trained once on one synthetic task, it transfers collapse-resistance to unseen task families (chance$\rightarrow$$0.71$/$0.60$ on CIFAR/Fashion). The layers best for in-context integration are not the layers best for weight-based consolidation, the late readout achieves higher accuracy and less forgetting at fewer parameters. The collapse is an integration failure at the vision--language interface, correctable by a lightweight, transferable intervention.

</details>


### [10] [CURV: Enhancing Chart Understanding Through Curriculum Visual Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02833)
*Xuehang Guo,Pingyue Zhang,Ruiyi Zhang,Zhenhailong Wang,Hanrui Lyu,Heng Ji,Tong Sun,Qingyun Wang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CURV框架，一个通过课程学习将图表问答（CQA）重新表述为多步骤视觉接地推理的框架，并配套引入三级课程数据集CCQA，从基础单操作推理逐步过渡到复杂多图表组合任务，实验证明该方法在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在图表问答中缺乏内在的视觉接地推理能力，虽然外在的思维链提示和视觉线索能提升性能，但模型仍存在感知不准确和推理链与视觉证据脱节的问题，亟需开发能够将逻辑推理与动态视觉接地相结合的内在推理能力。

Method: 提出CURV课程学习框架，将CQA重构为多步骤视觉接地推理过程，每一步通过空间注意力集中协调逻辑推理与动态视觉接地；同时引入CCQA三级课程数据集，通过可扩展的合成生成覆盖多样图表类型和推理模式，系统地从基础单操作推理逐步推进到复杂的多图表组合任务。

Result: CURV在基准上相较基线提升高达20.50%，在真实世界基准上提升达12.30%，在域外多模态推理任务上提升达10.20%，验证了内在视觉推理与动态接地的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉接地推理内化到模型中的课程学习方法，能够有效增强图表理解能力，并且具有良好的跨基准和跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Chart question answering (CQA) requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to integrate visual comprehension with logical reasoning, yet current models struggle with accurate visual grounding and coherent reasoning chains. While extrinsic chain-of-thought prompting and visual cues significantly improve performance, current MLLMs lack intrinsic visual grounded reasoning capabilities, leading to inaccurate perception and reasoning disconnected from visual evidence. To address these limitations, we propose CURV, a curriculum learning framework that develops intrinsic visual reasoning capabilities by reformulating CQA as multi-step visual grounded reasoning, where each step coordinates logical reasoning with dynamic visual grounding through spatial attention concentration. To assist model learning, we further introduce CCQA, a three-level curriculum dataset with scalable synthetic generation across diverse chart types and reasoning patterns. Our curriculum systematically progresses from basic single-operation reasoning to complex multi-chart compositional tasks. Experiments demonstrate that CURV achieves up to $\uparrow20.50\%$ improvements over baselines and is generalizable to real-world benchmarks (up to $\uparrow12.30\%$) and out-of-domain multimodal reasoning tasks (up to $\uparrow10.20\%$), validating the effectiveness of internalizing visual reasoning with dynamic grounding for enhanced chart understanding capabilities. Code is available at: https://xhguo7.github.io/CURV/.

</details>


### [11] [A Human-in-the-Loop Deep Learning Framework for Color Reconstruction of Lenticular Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02835)
*Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Tiago Kleist,Giorgio Trumpy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种人机协同（HITL）深度学习框架，用于柱状透镜胶片（如Kodacolor工艺）的彩色重建。该方法引入可编辑的矢量透镜边界表示，允许专家交互式修正边界位置后再进行颜色提取与去马赛克，并将重建色度与原扫描亮度融合以保留银盐乳剂中的纹理细节。实验表明，在自动化方法失效的挑战性胶片序列上，该方法能生成高质量、可展映的彩色重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史柱状透镜胶片（如Kodacolor）以特殊空间格式编码颜色信息，需要专门技术进行准确重建。现有自动化方法（如doLCE的信号处理方法与deep-doLCE的深度学习方法）在弯曲透镜、低对比度或拍摄质量差的区域常常失败，无法满足展映要求的色彩重建。

Method: 提出HITL深度学习框架，核心包括：(1) 可编辑的矢量透镜边界表示，允许专家交互式细化边界位置；(2) 解耦架构，将边界检测与颜色提取/去马赛克分离，支持针对性修正与迭代调优；(3) 亮度-色度融合策略，将重建色度与原始胶片扫描的亮度合并，保留仅存在于原始银盐乳剂中的图像细节。

Result: 在自动化方法失败的挑战性柱状透镜胶片序列上，该方法成功生成高质量、可展映的彩色重建，同时保留纹理细节。这是首个结合专家引导、可编辑中间表示和纹理保持后处理的柱状透镜胶片彩色重建工作。

Conclusion: 人机协同方法通过引入专家知识到检测模型中，显著提升了柱状透镜胶片彩色重建的鲁棒性，推动了该领域的最新进展；同时也印证了在复杂历史胶片修复任务中，纯粹自动化方法仍存在局限，人机结合是可行且有效的路径。

Abstract: Historical lenticular films, such as those created with the Kodacolor process, encode color information in a distinctive spatial format. This structure requires specialized techniques for accurate color reconstruction. While recent signal processing approaches like doLCE and deep learning methods like deep-doLCE have advanced automated color recovery, they often fail with cases such as curved lenticules, low-contrast, or badly captured regions. We propose a human-in-the-loop (HITL) deep learning framework which is designed for color reconstruction in lenticular films. Our approach introduces an editable, vector-based representation of lenticule boundaries, allowing experts to interactively refine boundary positions before color extraction and demosaicing. This decoupled architecture enables targeted corrections and iterative fine-tuning, embedding expert knowledge into the detection model and improving robustness across challenging frames. To preserve image details using information solely present in the original silver emulsion, we merge the reconstructed chrominance with the original film scan's luminance. We evaluate our pipeline on a challenging lenticular film sequence where previous automated approaches fail and the reconstructed colors are not suitable for exhibition. In contrast, our HITL approach successfully produces high-quality, exhibitable color reconstructions with preserved texture. This work is the first to combine expert guidance, editable intermediate representations, and texture-preserving post-processing for lenticular film color reconstruction, advancing the state of the art in this field.

</details>


### [12] [Localize, Don't Beautify: Client-Side Control of Image-Editing APIs for Cosmetic Surgery Previews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02841)
*Sukhrobbek Ilyosbekov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在仅使用客户端侧控制（不访问模型内部）的情况下，如何将商业图像编辑器的面部编辑限制在指定区域。通过对6种商业编辑配置和1种基于掩码的修复模型进行210次整容风格编辑测试，发现使用关键点推导的掩码进行"剪切-粘贴"合成（masked composite）能够显著改善编辑定位（定位指标中位数提高0.446），且不要求模型内部访问，适用于所有受测编辑器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业图像编辑器（如公共编辑API）在进行局部面部编辑时往往过度修改面部其他区域（如鼻部编辑同时改变皮肤或照明）。现有方法需要访问模型内部信息，而公共API不提供此能力。因此研究者想探究：仅从客户端侧出发，能实现多大程度的编辑控制。

Method: 研究设计了三个客户端侧控制级别：(1) 仅使用提示词；(2) 掩码合成——从模型中剪切编辑区域并通过关键点推导的掩码贴回原照片；(3) 若模型支持，让模型自身在掩码内进行修复。实验包含6种商业编辑配置和1个基于掩码的修复模型，执行下颌-颈部整容式编辑和鼻部整形，共进行210次编辑尝试。评估使用ArcFace余弦值衡量身份保持，CIELAB像素变化比率衡量编辑落在指定区域vs受保护面部区域的比例。

Result: 210次编辑中196次可评分。在12个可评分的正面脸部中，掩码合成相对仅提示词输出，定位指标中位数提高0.446（95%自举置信区间0.421-0.457），同时被请求区域的改变量大致不变。编辑器在编辑强度与身份保留上存在差异，且测试的修复模型并未优于简单合成方法。另外，基于每张脸的输入到术后基线比较，没有编辑器使输出在身份嵌入空间上更接近术后照片。

Conclusion: 将外科预览保持在其预期区域内不需要访问模型内部——客户端侧的掩码和合成方法即可在所有测试的编辑器上强制实现，且供应商成本低。但需注意本研究为控制性研究而非临床准确性研究：没有外科医生评估输出，每种条件仅生成一次。在身份保持方面，所有编辑器都未能使输出在身份嵌入上更接近术后照片。

Abstract: Ask a commercial image editor to preview a cosmetic procedure and it will often change more of the face than the request names: a nose edit can also smooth skin or alter lighting. Existing methods for confining an edit to one region require access to the model's internals, which a public editing API does not expose. We ask how much control is possible from the client side alone. In a pilot benchmark, six commercial editing configurations and one mask-based inpainting model perform facelift-style jaw-neck and rhinoplasty edits at three levels of client-side control: the prompt alone; cutting the edited region out of the response and pasting it back onto the original photograph through a landmark-derived mask (a masked composite); and asking the model itself to inpaint inside the mask where supported. Of 210 attempted edits, 196 could be scored. ArcFace cosine measures identity preservation; a CIELAB pixel-change ratio measures how much change lands inside the requested region rather than a protected facial zone. On the 12 frontal faces the regional metric could score, the masked composite improved localization over the paired prompt-only output by a median of 0.446 (95% face-clustered bootstrap interval 0.421-0.457) while changing the requested region about as much. Editors differed in edit strength versus identity retention, and the one inpainting model we tested did not beat the simple composite. Against each face's input-to-postoperative baseline, no editor moved its outputs closer to the postoperative photograph in identity-embedding terms. This is a study of control, not clinical accuracy: no surgeons rated the outputs, and each condition was generated once. Within that scope, keeping a surgical preview inside its intended region needs no access to the model; a mask and composite on the client enforce it across every editor tested, at low provider cost.

</details>


### [13] [Test Time Adaptation Methods for Point Cloud Registration in Laparoscopic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02883)
*Nina Bodelot,Soufiane Belharbi,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究3D点云配准中的测试时自适应（TTA）方法，针对腹腔镜手术中术前网格与术中重建点云之间的领域偏移问题，将三类TTA方法（模型、归一化、输入）改造适配于登记任务，并提出处理术前/术中非对称偏移的修改方案，在P2P和P2ILReg数据集上验证，发现输入自适应方法最能平衡推理延迟与配准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云配准中的监督网络仅在合成数据上训练，而真实腹腔镜重建数据存在噪声、稀疏、遮挡等问题导致领域偏移，严重退化对应关系估计性能。现有TTA方法主要依赖logits、熵、类别原型或缓存记忆，这些在配准任务中不可用，且配准涉及术前/术中成对输入的非对称偏移，缺乏面向该任务的TTA方法研究。

Method: 系统分析并改造了来自三类TTA家族（模型自适应、归一化自适应、输入自适应）的四种代表性方法：基于辅助任务模型更新、无反向传播的token清理、特征对齐、层归一化校准。核心修改包括：处理术前和术中点云间非对称偏移的适配机制，以及用配准友好的目标函数替代基于分类熵的目标。

Result: 在P2P和P2ILReg数据集上，使用干净合成源数据训练的基于对应关系的模型，对合成目标施加八种腐蚀（含均匀噪声和全局密度降低）五个严重等级，以及真实目标数据进行了评估。所有方法在P2P上均提升配准效果，但归一化自适应在P2ILReg上表现退化。综合考虑计算开销，输入自适应方法在腹腔镜手术场景下最具前景，推理延迟低且误差一致降低。

Conclusion: 输入自适应TTA是腹腔镜手术点云配准任务中最有潜力的TTA方案，能够在低推理延迟下保持一致的误差降低。不同TTA家族在不同数据集上的表现差异提示需要根据具体任务特性选择适配策略。

Abstract: 3D point cloud registration in laparoscopic surgery estimates the transformation between an intraoperative organ reconstructed from video and its preoperative mesh. Because ground-truth transformations are unavailable for real data, supervised networks are trained on synthetic organ pairs. At test time, real reconstructions differ from synthetic data and are noisy, sparse, and occluded, which degrades correspondence estimation. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can reduce this domain shift, but existing methods mainly rely on logits, entropy, class prototypes, or cache memories unavailable in registration. Registration also involves paired inputs with an asymmetric shift that primarily affects the intraoperative cloud. We analyse and modify state-of-the-art TTA methods from three families to 3D registration: model, normalization, and input adaptation. We analyze four representative approaches based on auxiliary-task model updates, backpropagation-free token purging, feature alignment, and layer-normalization calibration. We modify them to handle asymmetric shifts between preoperative and intraoperative point clouds and replace classification-based entropy objectives. Using a correspondence-based model trained on clean synthetic source data, we evaluate adaptation to corrupted synthetic and real target data on P2P and P2ILReg. For synthetic targets, we apply eight corruptions, including uniform noise and global density reduction, at five severity levels. All methods improve registration on P2P, whereas normalization adaptation degrades performance on P2ILReg. Considering the computational overhead of backpropagation-based adaptation, input adaptation is the most promising option for laparoscopic surgery, providing low inference latency and consistent error reductions across datasets. Code: https://github.com/ninaa-git/survey_pc_registration_tta

</details>


### [14] [Modeling Scientific Experiment Scenes: Dataset and Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02892)
*Minghao Zou,Qingtian Zeng,Shangkun Liu,Cong Liu,Paul L. Rosin,Guanghui Yue,Jun Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysScene——首个面向物理实验场景的场景图生成(SGG)数据集，并针对该数据集暴露的长尾谓词分布和视觉-文本语义鸿沟两大挑战，提出跨模态双路径生成器(CM-DPG)模型，在PhysScene和VG150上均取得具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGG基准主要聚焦日常图像，忽略了具有专业仪器、任务特定实验语义以及密集细粒度物理关系的科学实验场景。这类场景在自动化实验分析和智能教育中日益重要，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建PhysScene数据集，提供密集标注的场景图及多种监督/协议设置下的基准；2) 提出CM-DPG模型，通过联合视觉-文本编码增强对象级语义表示，利用互补的视觉与几何线索改进关系推理；3) 引入关系感知预训练、基于描述的伪监督和自适应加权策略，以支持头尾谓词的均衡学习。

Result: 在PhysScene和VG150上的大量实验表明，CM-DPG在多种评估设置下均取得具有竞争力的性能；消融研究验证了各组件的有效性。数据与代码已公开。

Conclusion: PhysScene填补了SGG在科学实验场景上的空白，CM-DPG有效应对了长尾谓词分布和视觉-文本语义鸿沟两大挑战，为开放词汇SGG在物理实验领域的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) is fundamental to structured visual understanding, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on daily life images and overlook scientific experiment scenes with specialized instruments, task-specific experimental semantics, and dense, fine-grained physical relations. These scenes are increasingly important for automated experimental analysis and smart education. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysScene, the first SGG dataset for physical experiment scenes, providing densely annotated scene graphs and benchmarks under multiple supervision and protocol settings. PhysScene further exposes two key algorithmic challenges for SGG: pronounced long-tail relational predicate distributions and a substantial visual-textual semantic gap. To address these challenges, we propose the Cross-Modal Dual-Path Generator (CM-DPG), a model for robust open-vocabulary SGG. The model enhances object-level semantic representations through joint visual-textual encoding and improves relational reasoning using complementary visual and geometric cues. We also incorporate relation-aware pre-training, caption-derived pseudo-supervision, and adaptive weighting to support balanced learning across head and tail predicates. Extensive experiments on PhysScene and VG150 show that CM-DPG achieves competitive performance across multiple evaluation settings, with ablation studies validating the contribution of each component. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/ZMH-SDUST/CM-DPG.

</details>


### [15] [RealWeather: Realistic and Scene-Faithful Weather Translation with Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02953)
*Yuwei Ning,Liangzhi Wang,Yi Xiao,Zhenhua Wu,Yun Pang,Mingkun Chan,Jichang Li,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RealWeather，一种驾驶世界模型，通过渐进式真实性引导（Progressive Realism Bootstrapping）和场景保真强化学习优化（Scene-Fidelity RL Optimization），实现真实且场景保真的天气迁移，无需配对视频数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实天气迁移对自动驾驶系统的开发与评估至关重要，但大规模收集同一场景下不同天气条件的配对视频不切实际。现有方法依赖合成数据、3D天气编辑或几何条件生成，往往在天气真实感或场景保真度上有所妥协。

Method: RealWeather包含两个核心组件：(1) Progressive Realism Bootstrapping——一种迭代数据精炼策略，借助辅助的Pseudo-Clear生成管线，训练初期使用伪风格条件视频，随后逐步用模型自身生成的越来越真实的视频替换这些输入，从而弥合伪域到真实域的差距，支持双向的清晰-恶劣天气迁移；(2) Scene-Fidelity RL Optimization——一种奖励驱动的策略优化方法，显式惩罚对安全关键驾驶要素的改动，以严格保证结构完整性并抑制幻觉。

Result: 大量实验表明，RealWeather在视觉真实感和结构保留方面显著优于现有方法，同时支持鲁棒的长尾天气场景生成和强大的零样本分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: RealWeather通过从真实世界视频中直接学习真实天气动态，结合渐进式训练策略和场景保真强化学习，有效解决了天气迁移中的真实感与场景保真之间的权衡问题，为自动驾驶系统的天气鲁棒性评估提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Realistic weather translation is valuable for developing and evaluating autonomous driving systems, yet collecting paired videos of the same scenes under different weather conditions at scale is impractical. Existing methods therefore rely on synthetic data, 3D weather editing, or geometry-conditioned generation, often compromising weather realism or scene fidelity. We propose RealWeather, a driving world model for both realistic and scene-faithful weather translation. Our key idea is to learn authentic weather dynamics directly from real-world videos. Specifically, RealWeather employs Progressive Realism Bootstrapping, an iterative data-refinement strategy. Assisted by an auxiliary Pseudo-Clear Generation pipeline, training initially starts with pseudo-style conditioning videos. As training proceeds, these inputs are progressively replaced with increasingly realistic videos generated by the model itself. This strategy bridges the pseudo-to-real domain gap, allowing the model to adapt seamlessly to real-world input distributions and naturally support bidirectional clear adverse translation. Furthermore, to strictly enforce structural integrity and suppress hallucinations, we introduce Scene-Fidelity RL Optimization, a reward-driven policy optimization strategy that explicitly penalizes alterations to safety-critical driving elements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RealWeather significantly outperforms existing methods in visual realism and structural preservation, while enabling robust long-tail weather scenario generation and strong zero-shot out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [16] [Material-Segmented Per-Pixel Emissivity Correction for Thermographic Anomaly Detection in Cultural Heritage Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02964)
*Jonathan Klingspon,Scott McAvoy,Maurizio Seracini,Falko Kuester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的长波红外测温校正管线，通过SAM 3.1开放词汇分割将共定位RGB图像分割为材料区域，映射到材料发射率表，并逐像素执行逆Planck求解以校正发射率变化引起的表观温度伪影。在物理合成基准上平均绝对误差从1.97K降至0.91K，但在遗产真实发射率分布下校正收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量长波热成像对遗产表面的测温受限于全局常数发射率假设，而异质表面上发射率在单一视场内变化，导致表观温度伪影既模拟又掩盖地下异常，需要逐像素发射率校正。

Method: 采用训练无关的管线：(1) 使用SAM 3.1开放词汇分割对共定位、共校准的RGB通道进行材料分割；(2) 将分割区域映射到基于一次测量文献编译的材料键控LWIR发射率表；(3) 将发射率场传播到原始辐射数据的逐像素逆Planck求解中。

Result: 在物理合成基准上，20K对比度下平均绝对误差从1.97K降至0.91K；在准确发射率表下，校正优于任何最佳拟合全局常数，但仅限于低发射率例外材料的测试布局；在遗产真实的发射率分布下（风化室外遗产材料发射率聚集在传统默认值附近），校正增益较小。

Conclusion: 校正效果集中在真正的低发射率例外材料上，对典型遗产表面收益有限。主要失败模式是开放词汇分割匹配外观而非材料，且存在发射率定义异常被抑制的禁忌场景。研究提供量化操作区间图和实测依据，对"遗产校正普遍有效"的说法提出审慎修正。

Abstract: Quantitative longwave thermography of heritage surfaces is limited by the global-constant emissivity assumption in inverse-Planck temperature retrieval; on heterogeneous surfaces emissivity varies within one field of view, producing apparent-temperature artifacts that mimic and mask subsurface anomalies. We present a training-free pipeline that derives per-pixel emissivity by applying SAM 3.1 open-vocabulary segmentation to a colocated, co-calibrated RGB channel, mapping segments to a material-keyed LWIR emissivity table compiled from primary measurement literature, and propagating the field into a per-pixel inverse-Planck solve on raw radiometric data. Lacking any public dataset with raw radiometry, a temperature reference, and a colocated RGB camera, we evaluate on a physics-based synthetic benchmark and four real datasets. On the benchmark, under a palette spanning the low-emissivity exceptions, the correction cuts mean absolute error from 1.97 K to 0.91 K at 20 K contrast and, with an accurate table, beats the best fitted global constant on every layout; on a heritage-realistic emissivity distribution it does not. We contribute a quantified operating-regime map, and a measurement-backed finding that tempers the heritage claim: weathered outdoor heritage emissivities cluster near the conventional default, so the correction is small on typical surfaces and concentrated on genuine low-emissivity exceptions. We characterize the dominant failure mode, in which open-vocabulary segmentation matches appearance rather than material, and the contraindicated regime in which emissivity-defined anomalies are suppressed.

</details>


### [17] [Qwen-3D: A Generalist 3D Vision-Language Model for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02980)
*Lucy Lin,Ayush Jain,Yifan Liu,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-3D，一种几何感知的大多模态模型（LMM），通过3D旋转位置编码（3D RoPE）和多视图几何线索将视觉信息压缩到统一3D场景空间中，并结合查询式分割解码器实现语言与3D几何的直接桥接，在3D基准和2D视觉-语言基准上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM在处理长视频时受限于帧中心式token化和有限上下文窗口，而3D几何（深度与相机位姿）为视觉流提供了天然压缩机制。虽然已有3D LMM利用几何感知表示提升空间推理能力，但在接地（grounding）和分割任务上仍落后于专用3D感知系统，根本原因在于现有方法通过语言token或轻量查询传递3D预测，造成了语言推理与密集几何预测之间的信息瓶颈。

Method: Qwen-3D采用以下核心设计：(1) 在Qwen骨干网络内利用多视角几何线索压缩视觉信息；(2) 为视觉token添加3D旋转位置嵌入（3D RoPE），使注意力直接作用于3D场景空间而非独立图像帧，实现跨视图和时间维度的可扩展推理；(3) 引入基于查询的分割解码器，将语言直接接地到底层3D场景表示，统一完成指称接地、实例分割和视觉问答任务；(4) 通过在2D和3D数据上联合训练，保持2D视觉-语言基准性能。

Result: 在一系列多样的基准测试中，Qwen-3D超越了现有3D LMM，并在多个任务上超过了大型专有2D模型；同时在标准2D视觉-语言基准上通过2D/3D联合训练保持强性能，实现了3D能力提升而2D能力不退化。

Conclusion: Qwen-3D验证了几何感知解码和3D场景空间注意力是突破LMM长视频理解瓶颈的关键路径，通过将语言推理与3D密集预测直接桥接，模型在不牺牲2D能力的前提下显著提升了3D空间理解和分割性能。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved remarkable success on images and short videos, yet scaling them to long videos remains challenging due to frame-centric tokenization and limited context windows. 3D geometry provides a natural compression mechanism for visual streams: depth and camera pose enable observations from multiple views and time steps to be fused into a persistent, world-aligned representation. While recent 3D LMMs leverage geometry-aware representations to improve spatial reasoning, they continue to lag behind specialist 3D perception systems on grounding and segmentation tasks. We argue that a key limitation is geometry-aware decoding: existing methods communicate 3D predictions through language tokens, proposal selection, or lightweight grounding queries, creating a bottleneck between language reasoning and dense geometric prediction. Building on these insights, we introduce Qwen-3D, a geometry-aware LMM that compresses visual information within the Qwen backbone using multi-view geometric cues, enabling efficient long-horizon visual reasoning over static scenes. Qwen-3D augments visual tokens with 3D Rotary Positional Embeddings, allowing attention to operate directly in 3D scene space rather than across independent image frames and thereby facilitating scalable cross-view and temporal reasoning. To bridge language and geometry, Qwen-3D incorporates a query-based segmentation decoder that grounds language directly in the underlying 3D scene representation, unifying referential grounding, instance segmentation, and visual question answering across both images and videos. Across a diverse set of benchmarks, Qwen-3D surpasses existing 3D LMMs and outperforms several large proprietary 2D models. Notably, Qwen-3D achieves these improvements while maintaining strong performance on standard 2D vision-language benchmarks by jointly training on 2D and 3D data.

</details>


### [18] [V-FIND: Revealing the Intrinsic Forgery Knowledge Encoded in Video Forgery Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03008)
*Shichao Kan,Chengpeng Hong,Jingtong Dou,Chuancheng Shi,Yuhan Liu,Linrui Xu,Yixiong Liang,Yigang Cen,Yanpeng Sun,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FIND框架，通过定位和激活视频伪造检测器内部稀疏的、功能专门化的鉴别神经元，在冻结主干网络的情况下仅训练轻量线性分类器即可实现跨多个外部基准的强检测性能，揭示视频伪造检测器包含稀疏、可提取、可复用的伪造鉴别知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频伪造检测研究通常将检测器视为黑盒进行完整模型重训练，计算资源消耗大，且未充分利用检测器内部潜在的伪造鉴别知识。作者质疑是否可以通过挖掘和激活检测器内部的稀疏法医知识来实现视频伪造检测，从而避免资源密集型的全模型重训练。

Method: V-FIND框架包含两步：首先定位真实与伪造视频之间差异显著的临界层（critical layers）；然后在临界层中识别持续携带伪造鉴别信号的潜在锚定神经元（anchor neurons），将其组织成紧凑的伪造子空间（forensic subspace）。保持原始骨干网络冻结，仅训练一个轻量线性分类器。

Result: 在多个外部生成视频基准上，冻结骨干网络并仅训练线性分类器的V-FIND子空间仍能实现强检测性能。进一步的神经元干预实验直接证明了所发现神经元的功能特异性。

Conclusion: 视频伪造检测器内部包含稀疏、可提取且可复用的伪造鉴别知识，这为理解和利用检测器内在法医能力提供了新视角，也为避免资源密集型全模型重训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As generated videos become increasingly realistic, reliable video forgery detection is increasingly important. Existing studies typically optimize and use video forgery detectors as black boxes, while the latent forgery-discriminative knowledge inside them remains largely unexplored. Instead of continuing to rely on resource-intensive full-model retraining to steadily improve detection performance, we ask whether video forgery detection can also be achieved by uncovering and activating sparse forensic knowledge within the detector. We find that forgery-discriminative knowledge is not uniformly distributed across the full representation space, but is concentrated in a sparse set of functionally specialized neurons. Based on this insight, we propose a video forgery-intrinsic neuron discovery (V-FIND) framework. V-FIND first localizes critical layers that exhibit pronounced discrepancies between real and forged videos, and then identifies latent anchor neurons that consistently carry forgery-discriminative signals, organizing them into a compact forensic subspace. With the original backbone frozen and only a lightweight linear classifier trained, this subspace still delivers strong detection performance across multiple external benchmarks for generated videos. Further neuron intervention experiments provide direct evidence for the functional specificity of the discovered neurons. Overall, these results suggest that video forgery detectors contain sparse, extractable, and reusable forgery-discriminative knowledge, offering a new perspective on understanding and exploiting their intrinsic forensic capability.

</details>


### [19] [Clinically-Grounded Hierarchical Classification for Consistent Chest X-ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03016)
*Jong Hak Moon,Minjun Kim,Minjun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASE框架，以统一的单阶段架构模拟放射科医生从粗到细的推理过程，通过三级层次化分类（9个解剖区域、17个子区域、28个病理发现）并联合优化多级监督、跨层级概率对齐和层级违例惩罚，实现胸部X光片的准确解释和层级一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片判读本质上是层次化的，临床决策不仅需要知道存在什么异常，还需要知道异常的位置，需要从大的解剖系统推理到具体的病理发现。然而现有自动化系统大多将其视为扁平分类问题，未能捕捉层级间依赖关系，也无法保证粗粒度与细粒度预测之间的一致性。

Method: CHASE采用共享Vision Transformer骨干网络，构建临床驱动的三级分类体系（9个解剖区域、17个子区域、28个病理发现），通过联合优化三项目标实现：多级监督学习、跨层级概率对齐以及层级违例惩罚，强制细粒度发现必须由粗粒度解剖上下文支持而非孤立预测。

Result: 实验表明CHASE在所有层级上均优于扁平与层次化基线方法，同时在概率层级一致性方面表现更优；逐层注意力图证实了预测具有解剖学依据。

Conclusion: CHASE验证了将放射科医生的粗到细推理模式显式建模到分类框架中，能同时提升分类准确性与层级一致性，为医学图像理解提供了具有临床可解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate chest X-ray interpretation is inherently hierarchical. Clinical decisions depend not only on what abnormality is present but where it is situated, requiring reasoning from broad anatomical systems down to specific pathological findings. Yet existing automated systems largely treat this as a flat classification problem, failing to capture inter-level dependencies or enforce coherence between coarse and fine predictions. We propose CHASE (Classification with Hierarchical Analysis and Structured Enforcement), a unified single-stage framework that mirrors radiologists' coarse-to-fine reasoning through a clinically driven three-level taxonomy of 9 anatomical regions, 17 sub-regions, and 28 pathological findings. CHASE jointly optimizes multi-level supervision, cross-level probability alignment, and a hierarchy-violation penalty within a shared Vision Transformer backbone. This ensures that fine-grained findings are anatomically supported by their coarser-level context rather than predicted in isolation. Experiments demonstrate that CHASE outperforms flat and hierarchical baselines across all levels while achieving superior probabilistic hierarchy consistency, with level-wise attention maps confirming anatomically grounded predictions. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/CHASE.

</details>


### [20] [Standalone DINOv3 for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03023)
*Changhao Zhao,Haoxiang Li,Yuke Li,Hai Liu,LingLin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DinoSplat-OV，一个基于DINOv3（DINO.txt）的训练-free开放词汇语义分割框架，专为遥感影像设计。通过Text-aware Laplacian Propagation模块（文本语义亲和+局部视觉相似去噪）和Gaussian Splatting Upsampling模块（RGB引导的各向异性聚合与测试时优化）实现像素级分割，并采用全局锚点滑动窗口策略支持大尺寸遥感图像，在UDD5、DOTA和LoveDA数据集上取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感语义分割因像素级标注成本高昂而受限，促使研究者探索免训练的开放词汇方法。DINOv3（DINO.txt）的出现使独立DINO骨干具备图像-文本对比学习能力，为开放词汇分割提供了新可能，但尚无工作将其适配到遥感领域。

Method: 提出训练-free框架DinoSplat-OV，包含两个核心模块：(1) Text-aware Laplacian Propagation模块，结合文本语义亲和力与局部视觉相似性对patch级预测去噪，增强区域一致性并保持边界；(2) Gaussian Splatting Upsampling模块，通过RGB引导的各向异性聚合和测试时优化重建像素级特征。另采用全局锚点滑动窗口策略处理大规模遥感图像。

Result: 在UDD5、DOTA和LoveDA三个遥感数据集上，DinoSplat-OV相对于现有训练-free方法表现出竞争力或更优的性能，填补了DINO系列模型在训练-free开放词汇分割领域的空白。

Conclusion: DinoSplat-OV有效弥合了DINO系列模型在训练-free开放词汇分割中的空白，为遥感开放词汇分割提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Remote sensing semantic segmentation is hindered by costly pixel-level annotations, motivating training-free open-vocabulary methods. Recently, the recent release of DINOv3 brings DINO.txt, which equips the standalone DINO backbone with image-text contrastive learning and thus opens up the possibility of open-vocabulary segmentation. We propose DinoSplat-OV, a training-free framework that adapts DINOv3 to remote sensing without fine-tuning or additional pretraining. Targeting the dense distribution, multi-scale nature, and large size of remote sensing imagery, we design two core modules. Its Text-aware Laplacian Propagation module de-noises patch-level predictions by combining textual semantic affinities with local visual similarity, improving regional consistency while preserving boundaries. Its Gaussian Splatting Upsampling module reconstructs pixel-level features through RGB-guided anisotropic aggregation and test-time optimization. A global-anchor sliding-window strategy further supports large-scale imagery. Experiments on UDD5, DOTA, and LoveDA demonstrate competitive or superior performance over existing training-free methods, effectively filling the gap of DINO-series models in training-free open-vocabulary segmentation and providing a viable new path for further advances in this direction.

</details>


### [21] [CAPE-T2V: Captioner-Anchored Prompt Enhancement toward Two-Sided Conditioning Alignment in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03046)
*Yizhuo Jia,Jingyun Hua,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPE-T2V框架，通过两步的"锚定提示增强"策略来缩小文本到视频（T2V）扩散Transformer（DiT）在推理时提示增强器（PE）输出与训练时详细视频字幕之间的分布差异（即PE-Caption gap）。第一步构建三类PE训练样本并微调PE；第二步用锚定PE重写的视频字幕微调DiT，推理时同一PE重写用户提示。在StoryEval、VBench-2.0和T2V-CompBench上超越基线，并通过平方最大均值差异（MMD）验证了更小的PE-Caption gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V扩散Transformer训练时使用详细视频字幕，但推理时依赖提示增强器（PE）重写的用户提示。即便在共享提示模式（schema）下，推理时的PE输出与训练时的视频字幕仍在细节选择、信息组织、描述粒度和措辞上存在差异，这种残余不匹配（PE-Caption gap）会损害生成质量，需要被系统性解决。

Method: CAPE-T2V包含两个步骤：(1) 构建三类PE训练样本——由字幕生成器（captioner）生成的目标字幕分别配对简洁源字幕、详细源字幕、以及从目标派生出的伪用户提示，微调PE使其将各输入映射到对应目标；(2) 使用锚定PE（Anchored PE）重写视频派生字幕来微调DiT，并在推理时用同一PE重写用户提示，实现两端条件对齐。

Result: 在Wan2.2和LTX-2.3上，相比采用相同字幕模式的基线，CAPE-T2V在StoryEval、VBench-2.0和T2V-CompBench上获得更高的聚合分数；同时其DiT微调字幕与推理时PE输出之间的分布距离（以固定嵌入空间中的平方MMD衡量）更小，验证了PE-Caption gap得到了有效缓解。

Conclusion: CAPE-T2V通过协同微调PE与DiT，实现了推理时PE输出和训练时字幕的双向条件对齐，有效缓解PE-Caption gap，是一种有效的T2V生成优化方案。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion transformers (DiTs) are trained with detailed video captions, whereas inference often relies on user prompts rewritten by a prompt enhancer (PE). Prior work has improved generation by optimizing the PE, the DiT, or both; some methods have also sought to narrow the training-inference mismatch through shared schemas. Yet even within a shared schema, inference-time PE outputs and DiT training captions may still differ in detail selection, information organization, descriptive granularity, and phrasing. We refer to this residual mismatch as the PE-Caption gap and introduce CAPE-T2V, a two-step Captioner-Anchored Prompt Enhancement framework toward two-sided conditioning alignment in T2V generation. First, CAPE-T2V constructs three types of PE training examples, pairing captioner-generated targets with concise source captions, detailed source captions, or pseudo user prompts derived from those targets. It then fine-tunes the PE to map each input to its paired target. Second, CAPE-T2V fine-tunes the DiT on video-derived captions rewritten by the Anchored PE; the same PE rewrites user prompts at inference. Relative to a baseline using the same caption schema, CAPE-T2V achieves higher aggregate scores on StoryEval, VBench-2.0, and T2V-CompBench across Wan2.2 and LTX-2.3. Further, CAPE-T2V exhibits a smaller PE-Caption gap than the baseline: its DiT fine-tuning captions are closer in distribution to inference-time PE outputs, as measured by squared maximum mean discrepancy in a fixed embedding space. Overall, these results support CAPE-T2V as an effective approach to mitigating the PE-Caption gap. The project is available at https://github.com/yizzz927/CAPE-T2V.

</details>


### [22] [AIDE: Automated Instruction via Distilled Expertise for Reference-Free Motor Skill Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03047)
*Yoshiki Ito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AIDE框架，仅利用专家参考序列进行训练，推理时仅需学习者姿态序列即可生成自然语言动作技能反馈，在ExpertAF数据集上优于无参考基线，效果接近需要完整专家演示的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动技能学习中，自然语言辅导反馈能加速学习，但专家教练稀缺且昂贵。现有基于参考的方法在训练和推理时都需要专家示范，限制了实际部署。因此需要一种只在训练阶段利用专家参考、推理时无需专家演示即可生成反馈的方法。

Method: AIDE包含教师模型和学生模型两阶段：教师模型通过冻结的语言模型从配对的(学习者-专家)姿态中学习生成反馈，产生独立的学习者token和编码学习者-专家差异的差异token；学生模型继承教师的编码器和权重初始化，用辅助模块从学习者姿态单独生成互补token，替代显式的专家对比。

Result: 在ExpertAF数据集上，AIDE在大多数指标上优于无参考基线，性能与训练和推理阶段都需要专家演示的方法相当，LLM评估也支持这些结果。

Conclusion: AIDE成功实现了推理阶段无需专家参考即可生成高质量的自然语言动作技能反馈，兼顾了部署便利性和反馈质量，为运动技能学习辅导的自动化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generating natural-language coaching feedback on motor skills can accelerate learning, yet expert coaches are scarce and expensive. Existing reference-based methods require expert demonstrations at both training and inference time, limiting practical deployment. We propose AIDE (Automated Instruction via Distilled Expertise), a framework that exploits expert references only during training and generates feedback from a learner's pose sequence alone at inference. A teacher model first learns to generate feedback from paired learner-expert poses via a frozen language model, producing separate learner tokens and difference tokens that encode the learner-expert difference. A student model then inherits the teacher's encoder and weight initialization, replacing the explicit expert comparison with an auxiliary module that produces complementary tokens from the learner's pose alone. On the ExpertAF dataset, AIDE outperforms reference-free baselines on most metrics and performs comparably to methods requiring expert demonstrations at both training and inference, with LLM-based evaluation supporting these findings.

</details>


### [23] [PDD-RRG: Posterior Diagnostic Decision for Study-level Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03055)
*Yang Yu,Yiming Ji,Bin Dai,Dong Zhang,Zhiyong Zhou,Shoushan Li,Yakang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PDD-RRG的后验诊断决策框架，用于自动放射学报告生成。该框架在报告生成后引入决策阶段，通过创建多种输入数据子集、利用贝叶斯后验概率和学得的阈值来整合潜在冲突的诊断，从而在不重新训练任何现有模型的情况下提升临床效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRG方法未能充分利用检查相关的全部信息，尽管一些工作尝试整合多视图图像和历史数据，但这些额外输入有时反而会导致可避免的诊断错误。因此需要一种方法能在报告生成阶段之后整合潜在冲突的诊断，提高临床准确性。

Method: PDD-RRG框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 创建多种输入数据子集，利用现有RRG模型从不同视角生成报告；(2) 对每个临床观察计算贝叶斯后验概率和学得的阈值，得到聚合的诊断结论，并用该结论来精炼生成的报告。整个过程无需重新训练现有模型。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上的实验表明，所提出的PDD-RRG框架能够有效提升现有RRG模型的临床效能，且无需任何重新训练。

Conclusion: 首次在报告生成后引入决策阶段来处理多源信息冲突问题，通过贝叶斯后验概率聚合各视角诊断结论来精炼最终报告，为RRG提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的增强方案。

Abstract: Automatic radiology report generation (RRG) aims to simulate the workflow of radiologists, assisting them in clinical diagnosis. However, existing methods often fall short in utilizing all information relevant to the examination, as is typically done in clinical practice. Although some works attempt to incorporate multi-view images and historical data, these additional inputs may sometimes lead to avoidable diagnostic errors on the contrary. To address these challenges, we introduce a decision-making stage after report generation for the first time and propose a Posterior Diagnostic Decision framework (PDD-RRG) to integrate potentially conflicting diagnoses. Specifically, we create various subsets of input data and utilize an existing RRG model to generate reports from different perspectives. Then the Bayesian posterior probability and the learned thresholds for each clinical observation are calculated to obtain an aggregated diagnostic conclusion, which is subsequently used to refine the generated report. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR demonstrate that our proposed PDD-RRG can effectively enhance the clinical efficacy of existing RRG models without any retraining.

</details>


### [24] [TASQ: Temporal-Adaptive Bit Sparsification Quantization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03057)
*Seokho Han,Dongwei Wang,Jinhee Kim,Yiran Chen,Kang Eun Jeon,Huanrui Yang,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASQ提出了一种时间自适应位稀疏量化方法，通过共享最大精度权重缓冲区和学习时间-空间LSB掩码，在不同去噪阶段为每层选择较低的有效精度，从而在不增加存储开销的情况下减少计算量，配合时间精度引擎实现50%以上的执行周期缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态量化在去噪过程的所有步骤中使用统一的权重精度。为了保证质量，精度必须满足最敏感的量化步骤，即使其他许多步骤可以容忍更少的比特。这导致模型在去噪轨迹中反复进行最坏情况的计算，尽管内存预算被满足，但算术成本持续过高。

Method: TASQ将存储成本和计算成本分离：1）存储一个共享的最大精度权重缓冲区；2）学习一个Temporal-Spatial LSB Mask，通过截断最低有效位为每层和每个去噪阶段选择较低的有效精度；3）引入Temporal-Precision Engine将学习到的调度映射到位串行执行，循环数与有效精度成正比，切换精度无额外的循环开销。

Result: 在PixArt-Sigma、SANA-1.6B和SDXL-Turbo上，TASQ实现了与静态量化相当的质量但计算量更少。结合Temporal-Precision Engine，相比静态量化减少了25%到50%的执行周期，相比朴素的静态8位位串行执行减少了6.1到7.5倍。

Conclusion: TASQ通过时间自适应位稀疏量化成功解耦了存储成本与计算成本，在保持模型质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，为扩散模型的量化推理提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Static quantization assigns one weight precision to every denoising step. To preserve quality, that precision must accommodate the most quantization-sensitive step, even though many other steps can tolerate fewer bits. The resulting model may satisfy its memory budget, but it repeatedly pays worst-case arithmetic throughout the denoising trajectory. We introduce Temporal-Adaptive Bit Sparsification Quantization (TASQ) to separate these two costs. TASQ stores one shared maximum-precision weight buffer and learns a Temporal-Spatial LSB Mask that selects a lower effective precision for each layer and denoising stage by truncating least-significant bits. Storage therefore remains fixed by the worst case, while BitOPs decrease at less sensitive stages without per-stage weight copies or runtime search. A Temporal-Precision Engine maps the learned schedule to bit-serial execution, where cycles scale with effective precision and switching precision has no measured cycle overhead. On PixArt-Sigma, SANA-1.6B, and SDXL-Turbo, TASQ achieves quality comparable to static quantization with less computation. Together with the Temporal-Precision Engine, it reduces execution cycles by 25 to 50 percent over static quantization and by 6.1 to 7.5x over a naive static 8-bit bit-serial execution. Code is available at https://github.com/seokho-han/tasq.

</details>


### [25] [RIDGE: Re-Noising with Internal Dynamic Guidance for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03059)
*Ruiliang Gong,Zhen Wang,Yanghao Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RIDGE（Re-Noising with Internal Dynamic Guidance），一种无反转、免训练的基于流的图像编辑方法。它通过重新加噪来维持编辑状态作为不可用干净目标状态的渐进近似，使噪声位移随噪声水平自然收缩；并在早期高噪声步骤中利用模型内部生成的动态掩码进行引导，在SD3 Medium和FLUX.1-dev两个骨干上取得了源保真度、目标对齐和感知质量之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无反转流式图像编辑方法在每个编辑步骤都需要目标侧状态，常用的等位移构造在不同噪声水平下保持源状态与目标状态之间的位移不变，这与加噪过程的物理特性不一致——干净状态下两个状态的位移应随噪声水平增加而收缩。因此这种构造在高噪声阶段会导致过于激进的更新，影响编辑质量。

Method: RIDGE将编辑状态视为不可用干净目标状态的动态近似：1) 使用与干净源状态相同的噪声水平和噪声样本对该近似重新加噪，使噪声位移随噪声增加自然收缩，避免等位移构造的不一致性问题；2) 在早期高噪声步骤中应用内部动态引导（Internal Dynamic Guidance），利用干净目标状态的预测来引导临时编辑状态，通过从模型内部生成的软动态掩码将引导聚焦于需要修改的区域，无需外部分割或检测模型。

Result: 在两个基准测试、两个骨干模型（SD3 Medium和FLUX.1-dev）上的实验表明，RIDGE在源保真度、目标对齐和感知质量三个维度之间取得了有利的总体权衡。

Conclusion: RIDGE通过重新加噪策略解决了等位移构造与加噪过程不一致的问题，并利用模型内部动态引导实现无需外部模型的精准区域引导，为无反转流式图像编辑提供了一种更符合噪声动态特性的有效方法。

Abstract: Inversion-free flow-based image editing avoids latent inversion, but still requires a target-side state at every editing step. The widely used equal-displacement construction keeps the displacement between the noisy source state and the target-side state unchanged across noise levels. This is inconsistent with noising, under which the displacement between two clean states noised with the same noise level and noise sample should contract as the noise level increases. Thus, it can lead to overly aggressive updates at high noise levels. We introduce RIDGE: Re-Noising with Internal Dynamic Guidance for Image Editing, an inversion-free and training-free method that maintains the edited state as an evolving approximation to the unavailable clean target state. RIDGE re-noises this approximation using the same noise level and noise sample as the clean source state, allowing their noisy displacement to decrease naturally with increasing noise. Since the edited state initially contains limited target semantics, RIDGE further applies internal dynamic guidance during the early high-noise steps. A clean target state prediction guides the provisional edited state through a soft dynamic mask derived internally from the model, focusing guidance on regions that require modification without external segmentation or detection models. Experiments on two benchmarks using two backbones, SD3 Medium and FLUX.1-dev, show that RIDGE offers a favorable aggregate trade-off among source preservation, target alignment, and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [26] [Global Graph-Validated Optimization for VLM-based 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03064)
*Jialu Huang,Yingxuan You,Fei Wang,Zheng Dang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图中间表示和混合搜索-细化策略的开放词汇3D室内布局生成方法，通过全局语义验证（GSV）和全局物理可行性搜索（GPFS）解决现有工作在全局一致性和物理可行性上的不足，在语义一致性和物理合理性方面达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D室内布局生成方法大多隐式建模资产间关系，或依赖局部成对约束和局部优化，这些方法与全局、高度非凸的布局空间不一致，常常生成局部看似合理但全局不一致或物理上不可行的场景。

Method: 提出图基中间表示将语义一致性与物理可行性分离。具体包括两个模块：(1) 全局语义验证（GSV），将场景表示为结构化图，通过基于规则的验证强制执行语义约束，消除矛盾配置，生成全局一致的语义脚手架；(2) 全局物理可行性搜索（GPFS），结合进化搜索进行全局探索和梯度细化进行局部利用，减少对VLM初始化的依赖，提高在非凸和不连续可行空间中的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在开放词汇3D室内布局生成任务上达到最先进的性能，同时提升了语义一致性和物理合理性。

Conclusion: GSV和GPFS将布局生成从局部关系建模和初始化敏感的优化推进到全局一致性推理和搜索，有效解决了全局语义验证与物理可行性搜索的联合优化问题。

Abstract: We study open-vocabulary 3D indoor layout generation, which synthesizes diverse and physically plausible scenes from unlabeled 3D assets using free-form language instructions. Recent methods leverage large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) to generate structured scenes from text. However, most model inter-asset relations implicitly or rely on local pairwise constraints and local optimization. These formulations are poorly aligned with the global, highly non-convex layout space, often yielding locally plausible yet globally inconsistent or physically infeasible scenes. We address this problem with a graph-based intermediate representation that separates semantic coherence from physical feasibility, together with a hybrid search-and-refinement strategy. First, Global Semantic Verification (GSV) represents scenes as structured graphs and enforces semantic constraints through rule-based verification. This explicit validation removes contradictory configurations and produces a globally consistent semantic scaffold. Second, Global Physical Feasibility Search (GPFS) combines evolutionary search for global exploration with gradient-based refinement for local exploitation. It reduces dependence on VLM-proposed initialization and improves robustness in non-convex and discontinuous feasible spaces. Together, GSV and GPFS move layout generation beyond local relational modeling and initialization-sensitive optimization toward globally consistent reasoning and search. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary 3D indoor layout generation, improving both semantic consistency and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [27] [LDU-Bench: Multimodal LLM Evaluation for Lithography Defect Understanding under Layout-Varying Circuit Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03078)
*Huanglong Ji,Botong Zhao,Shujing Lv,Yue Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LDU-Bench，一个面向光刻缺陷理解的多任务多模态基准测试平台，将光刻复检流程分解为缺陷分诊、形态识别、粗定位和图像条件原因分析四个独立任务，并引入Lithography Closure Score（LCS）进行系统评估。实验发现现有MLLM在缺陷分诊上表现可靠，但在下游复检阶段（形态对齐、有效定位、证据到原因映射）存在能力断层，反映了跨语义层面结构化理解的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测中，多模态大语言模型已具备较强的缺陷识别能力，但在光刻复检场景下，仅判断图像中是否存在缺陷不足以满足工程检测需求。模型还必须理解缺陷形态、空间位置以及由可见证据支持的可能成因。现有研究缺乏一个能够系统评估这些能力的多任务基准平台。

Method: 从真实光刻和集成电路复检图像构建LDU-Bench基准，将复检工作流分解为四个独立任务：缺陷分诊（defect triage）、形态识别（morphology recognition）、粗定位（coarse localization）和图像条件原因分析（image-conditioned cause analysis）。采用任务级指标、诊断读数（diagnostic readouts）和Lithography Closure Score（LCS）进行系统评估。

Result: 实验表明：现有MLLM能够在缺陷分诊任务上表现相对可靠，但这种能力无法稳定迁移到下游复检阶段；形态对齐、有效定位和证据到原因映射仍是主要瓶颈；进一步的诊断显示这种能力断层并非单一指标的波动，而是反映了模型在跨语义层面上结构化理解的不足。

Conclusion: LDU-Bench提供了可量化、可诊断的统一平台，用于评估工业MLLM在光刻复检链路中的可用性、失败点与能力边界，为后续模型改进提供了明确的评测依据。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong defect recognition capability in industrial anomaly detection. However, in lithography review, merely determining whether an image contains a defect is insufficient for engineering inspection; models must also understand defect morphology, spatial location, and the potential causes supported by visible evidence. To this end, this paper proposes LDU-Bench, a multi-task multimodal benchmark for lithography defect understanding. Constructed from real lithography and integrated-circuit review images, LDU-Bench decomposes the review workflow into four independent tasks: defect triage, morphology recognition, coarse localization, and image-conditioned cause analysis. It systematically evaluates models using task-level metrics, diagnostic readouts, and the Lithography Closure Score (LCS). Experimental results show that although existing MLLMs can perform defect triage relatively reliably, this ability does not stably transfer to downstream review stages. Morphology alignment, effective localization, and evidence-to-cause mapping remain the major bottlenecks. Further diagnostics indicate that this capability break is not a fluctuation of a single metric, but reflects insufficient structured understanding across semantic levels. Overall, LDU-Bench provides a quantifiable and diagnostic unified platform for evaluating the usability, failure points, and capability boundaries of industrial MLLMs in lithography review chains.

</details>
